### [1] [From Relevant Posts to Attributable Spans: Explicit Symptom Evidence for Depression Questionnaire Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12900)
*Sumanth Manduru,Carlotta Domeniconi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一套以可归因、症状相关证据为基础的抑郁问卷推断流程；实验显示，它能以更少帖子检索到更准确的证据，并提升问卷条目回答的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体长期记录有望帮助评估抑郁状况，甚至推断标准化问卷的回答。但现有方法主要依据语义相似度检索帖子，可能纳入无法归因于用户本人或与特定症状不够相关的内容，尤其容易误读同时涉及多种症状的帖子，以及用户转述他人经历的讨论。因此，需要先定位足够具体且确属用户本人的证据，再据此推断问卷答案。

Method: 研究提出一套名为“症状检索与证据支持推断”的流程。首先，从文本中提取以第一人称表达、且针对具体症状的片段；其次，借鉴设施选址目标，从候选证据中挑选具有代表性的子集；最后，通过证据门控推断，依据所选证据预测问卷回答。研究分别在抑郁症状证据检索数据集和多个年度的社交媒体风险评估数据集上评测检索效果与证据条件下的问卷推断效果。

Result: 与先进检索方法相比，该流程的帖子级精确率相对提升百分之七十，调和平均数相对提升百分之五十九，同时使用的帖子至少减少百分之三十。在包含多种症状的句子上，即使检索预算是稠密检索方法的两倍，该方法的精确率和调和平均数仍更高。在三个年度的数据集上，它的问卷条目完全一致率最高，在有序评分和汇总指标上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 研究表明，抑郁问卷推断不仅取决于能否找到语义相关的内容，还取决于证据是否确实归属于用户、是否针对具体症状，以及证据集合如何构成。先定位并筛选可靠证据，再进行推断，有助于提高回答准确性并减少无关信息带来的误导。

Abstract: Longitudinal social-media histories offer an opportunity to assess depression, potentially supporting the inference of standardized depression questionnaire responses. However, this task requires evidence that is semantically relevant to a symptom and attributable to the user, while being sufficiently specific to support an individual questionnaire item. Existing approaches rely on semantic similarity to retrieve posts, which can introduce misleading or non-attributable evidence, particularly in multi-symptom posts and discussions of others' experiences. We propose SREE (Symptom Retrieval and Evidence-Grounded InfErence), an evidence-grounded pipeline that extracts first-person and symptom-specific text spans, selects a representative subset using a Facility Location-inspired objective, and performs Evidence-Gated Inference over the selected evidence. We evaluate evidence retrieval on BDI-Sen and evidence-conditioned questionnaire inference on eRisk 2019-2021. Compared with state-of-the-art retrieval approaches, SREE achieves relative improvements of 70% in post-level precision and 59% in F1, while retaining at least 30% fewer posts. On multi-symptom sentences, it also outperforms dense retrieval in precision and F1 even with twice the retrieval budget. Across all three eRisk collections, SREE achieves the highest exact item-level agreement while remaining competitive on ordinal and aggregate metrics. These results highlight the importance of localizing attributable evidence and controlling its composition before inferring depression questionnaire responses.

</details>


### [2] [Rethinking in Spikes: Mitigating Hallucinations in MDLMs with Step-Aware Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12901)
*Zhongxing Xu,Zhonghua Wang,Zhe Qian,Shiyan Su,Ming Hu,Xiaocheng Zou,Wei Feng,Mingquan Lin,Yuyin Zhou,Yifan Peng,Hamid Rezatofighi,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可插拔的解码策略：在扩散过程中检测异常熵峰，并暂时保留候选语义分布进行连续精炼，以减少多模态模型的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态扩散语言模型在迭代去噪中整体不确定性通常逐渐下降，但少数步骤会出现局部熵峰，这些异常与幻觉密切相关。常规离散令牌提交会丢弃被遮蔽位置的分布信息，使候选语义难以在异常步骤中继续与上下文交互，因此需要一种能保留并利用这些信息的解码方式。

Method: 提出“步进精炼”策略，根据每个去噪步骤相对于邻近时间窗口的熵偏差识别异常步骤。在熵峰处，模型使用按概率加权的嵌入表示候选令牌，保留多种推理假设，并进行多轮连续精炼；控制器同时监测分布稳定性，在精炼收益不足或分布趋稳时停止循环，切回标准离散去噪。

Result: 摘要报告称，该方法在多个多模态基准测试和不同扩散语言模型上进行了广泛实验，并显著缓解了幻觉。摘要未提供具体数据或基线比较细节。

Conclusion: 利用熵异常定位高风险去噪步骤，并在这些步骤中暂缓离散提交、借助语义上下文持续精炼分布，是提升多模态扩散语言模型可靠性的有效思路。该方法兼顾候选信息保留与计算控制，可作为现有解码流程的可插拔改进。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal diffusion language models (MDLMs) have exhibited strong global modeling capabilities through iterative refinement. We observe that mask uncertainty follows an overall downward trend during iterative denoising, while a few denoising steps exhibit localized entropy spikes closely associated with hallucinations. We argue that anomalous steps can be identified by measuring the entropy deviation of each denoising step from its neighboring temporal window. We hypothesize that discrete token commitment discards the distributional information at masked positions, limiting the continued interaction between candidate semantics and the context during entropy spikes. With this goal, we present StepRefine, a plug-and-play decoding strategy that leverages semantic context to achieve reliable denoising. The core of our method lies in a step-aware switch between discrete denoising and latent refinement. The model employs probability-weighted embeddings at entropy spike steps to preserve diverse reasoning hypotheses and performs multiple rounds of continuous refinement. Meanwhile, a refinement controller monitors distributional stability to reduce unnecessary loops and switch the model back to standard discrete denoising. Through extensive experiments, StepRefine demonstrates significant hallucination mitigation across different MDLMs on multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [3] [TimeOmni-o1: Bidirectionally Coupling Time Series Foundation Models and LLMs for Multimodal Reasoning and Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12902)
*Ziyang Zhang,Shenyi Li,Wen Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出时间全模态一号模型，将时间序列基础模型与大语言模型双向耦合，使其能够联合理解文本与序列、以文本进行推理，并生成预测序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的时间序列常伴随事件报告等文本信息，理解和预测任务都需要结合两种模态推理。然而，大语言模型难以处理长序列；时间序列基础模型虽擅长预测，却通常无法读写文本；现有时间序列语言模型则未能充分利用基础模型已有的预测能力。

Method: 模型将预训练时间序列基础模型与大语言模型双向连接：前者把序列压缩编码后交给后者，使其结合文本进行推理；随后将语言模型的推理结果反馈给时间序列基础模型，由后者据此生成预测。研究还构建了多模态时间序列推理数据集，涵盖十二个领域、五十多万个样本，并提供标注推理过程，覆盖理解与预测任务。

Result: 在该数据集上，所提模型在大多数领域的理解与预测任务中优于现有模型，也优于其内部的大语言模型或时间序列基础模型单独使用时的表现。在三个公开基准上，模型同样超过专用时间序列模型。

Conclusion: 双向结合语言推理能力与时间序列预训练预测能力，有助于统一处理文本和时间序列的理解与生成。论文结果显示，这种架构及配套推理数据能够提升多领域任务表现，并具备向公开基准迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Time series from real-world systems are often accompanied by text, such as a report on an event that affects them. Two common tasks on these series are understanding, e.g., answering questions about a series, and forecasting its future values. Both tasks require reasoning over the series and the text together. Moreover, understanding outputs text while forecasting outputs a time series, so serving both requires a model that reads and generates both modalities. Large language models (LLMs) read time series as digit tokens and handle long series poorly. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast well from large-scale pretraining but typically neither read nor generate text. Time series language models add a time series modality to the LLM but forecast on the LLM side, leaving the TSFM's pretrained forecasting ability unused. We present TimeOmni-o1, which couples a pretrained TSFM and an LLM bidirectionally. In one direction, the TSFM encodes the series in compressed form for the LLM, which reasons over it together with the text. In the other, the LLM's reasoning is fed back into the TSFM, which produces the forecast conditioned on it. One model architecture thus reads and generates both series and text, reasons in text, and draws on the TSFM's pretrained forecasting ability. To train it, we build MMTR, a multimodal time series reasoning dataset with annotated reasoning traces, covering both tasks across 12 domains with 500k+ samples. On MMTR, TimeOmni-o1 outperforms existing models on both understanding and forecasting across most domains, as well as the LLM and the TSFM inside it when used individually. The gains carry over to three open benchmarks, where it outperforms the dedicated time series models.

</details>


### [4] [SELD: Detecting Silent Evidence Loss in Context Compression beyond the Answer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12903)
*Wentao Lei,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练和标注的检测方法，可发现压缩上下文中关键证据被删除、但模型答案未改变的隐性证据损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩质量检查通常比较压缩前后的答案或模型最可能输出的词，因此可能漏掉一种常见风险：关键证据已被删去，但答案仍然相同。这样的答案可能失去依据，也会误导后续复用压缩上下文的模型。

Method: 研究提出“隐性证据损失检测器”。其核心依据是：证据丢失时，即使模型给出的答案不变，它对未选答案的偏好也可能发生变化。该方法比较压缩前后模型对其他候选答案的偏好，无需训练或标注。研究还构建了“隐性证据损失配对”基准，在三个问答数据集中设置长度匹配的证据删除与对照删除样例。

Result: 在五种阅读模型上的定量与定性实验显示，该方法在不同压缩器、压缩率和模型条件下均优于强基线。在答案保持不变的配对样例中，它有百分之八十三的概率识别出删除证据的编辑；最强基线为百分之七十。

Conclusion: 即使压缩前后答案一致，压缩上下文中的证据损失仍可通过模型对未选答案的偏好变化加以检测。这为评估上下文压缩是否保留答案依据提供了不同于答案一致性检查的有效途径。

Abstract: Context compression is now routine for language models with long inputs, but a compressed context is useful only if it keeps the evidence for the answer. Existing checks compare the reader model's answer or its most likely words, and thus miss a frequent failure, silent evidence loss: key evidence is removed while the answer stays the same, leaving the answer unsupported and risky for other models that reuse the context. To address this challenge, we propose SELD (Silent Evidence Loss Detector), which needs no training and no labels. Its principle is that when evidence is lost, a reader model's preferences among the answers it leaves unsaid shift even if its answer does not, so comparing these preferences before and after compression reveals losses that answer checks cannot see. We also build SEL-Pairs, a benchmark of length-matched evidence and control deletions from three question-answering datasets. Quantitative and qualitative experiments with five reader models on it show that SELD consistently outperforms strong and recent baselines across compressors, compression rates and models, identifying the evidence-removing edit in 83% of matched pairs with unchanged answers, against 70% for the strongest baseline. These results show that evidence loss in compressed contexts can be detected even when the answer is unchanged.

</details>


### [5] [Actionable Feedback: Improving Model Capabilities from User Edit Data on Clinical Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12904)
*Daniel P. Jeong,Yewon Byun,John Giorgi,Peng Xu,Zachary C. Lipton,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究如何利用临床医生对大语言模型生成的出院摘要所做的修改来改进模型，同时避免模型学习到缺乏输入依据的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床医生的修改看似能为模型训练提供大量监督信号，但并非所有修改都能从模型输入中得到支持。对实际部署数据的审查发现，超过百分之四十五的经修改输出含有输入未直接支持的新增信息。若直接使用这些修改进行微调，可能使模型更容易生成无依据内容。

Method: 研究首先依据真实临床文档工具的使用数据，建立临床医生修改行为分类体系；随后在公开的重症监护临床数据集上构造半合成修改数据，用于出院摘要生成任务；最后提出简单的筛选方法，识别有依据、可用于训练的修改，并通过专家审查评估筛选准确性。

Result: 在半合成数据上的实验显示，直接用未经筛选的修改结果微调会增加无依据内容。逐步筛选并保留有输入依据的修改，则能改善模型与临床医生偏好的一致性指标，同时保持对输入内容的忠实性。

Conclusion: 临床编辑数据并非天然可靠的训练信号。通过刻画修改行为并筛选有依据的编辑，可以更安全地利用临床工作流中的反馈，提升模型与临床实践的契合度，而不牺牲内容忠实性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed in clinical documentation workflows, where clinicians review and edit their outputs before use. In principle, these clinician edits represent a scalable source of supervision for model training. In practice, not all edits represent actionable feedback: for instance, on data drawn from real-world deployment of an AI clinical documentation product, our review suggests that over 45% of edited outputs contain user-added information that is not directly supported by the inputs to the model. Naively fine-tuning on such edits risks teaching the model to hallucinate. In this paper, we develop a method to address this challenge. Given that real clinician edits on deployed documentation tools are not publicly available, we first develop a taxonomy of clinician-editing behaviors, informed by data from a deployed AI clinical documentation product. Then, we develop a method for generating semi-synthetic clinician-edit data, informed by our taxonomy, on a discharge-summary generation task based on MIMIC-III, a public dataset. Finally, we introduce simple filtering methods for identifying actionable edits, with accuracy evaluated by expert review. We show empirically, on our semi-synthetic dataset, that naive fine-tuning on raw edited output increases ungrounded content, while our approach of progressively filtering toward grounded edits enables improvement on clinician-alignment metrics while retaining faithfulness to the input context.

</details>


### [6] [Brasilidade: Measuring Brazilian Identity for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12906)
*José Roberto Homeli da Silva,Felipe Jorge do Nascimento,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,William Alberto Cruz Castañeda,Marcellus Amadeus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出巴西葡萄牙语评测基准“巴西性”，并发现模型规模及葡萄牙语专项训练都不能可靠预测模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评测方法难以充分衡量模型对巴西葡萄牙语的掌握情况，尤其是语言细微差异、语法特点和地区变体。因此，研究旨在建立更贴合巴西葡萄牙语实际特征的评测基准，揭示模型能力短板并为后续训练提供依据。

Method: 研究提出一套标准化评测方法，涵盖四个领域，并开放了第一领域的任务。该领域主要考查巴西葡萄牙语的语言细节、语法和变体。研究通过不同难度的任务协议评估多种模型，并比较其表现。

Result: 实验显示，模型规模并非决定表现的因素，部分中等规模模型在评测中超过了更大型模型。面向葡萄牙语专项训练的模型总体上也未能达到平均水平。不同任务的难度差异明显：有些任务对多数模型较为容易，有些极具挑战，另一些则呈现较大的模型间表现差异。

Conclusion: 研究表明，针对巴西葡萄牙语特点设计专门基准十分必要。此类评测能够更细致地揭示模型能力边界，帮助后续训练针对性地弥补不足，并推动模型更好地处理巴西葡萄牙语。

Abstract: This work presents a new benchmark that aims to address the limitations of current methods for evaluating LLMs in Brazilian Portuguese. We introduced Brasilidade, an assessment methodology comprised of standardized tasks distributed across four different domains, and we are making available the tasks from its first domain, which address nuances of the language, grammar, and variations of Brazilian Portuguese. The results show that model size, by itself, does not determine performance; several medium-sized models outperformed larger models in the protocols evaluated. Specific models for Portuguese, in general, did not perform above average, despite their targeted training. Different task levels revealed significant nuances: while some tasks proved easy for most models, others posed extreme challenges, and some yielded mixed results, with wide variations in performance. This clearly illustrates the need for benchmarks that address the limitations of the models in Brazilian Portuguese, allowing future training cycles to seek to overcome them. Project repository https://github.com/soberania-oficial/brasilidade

</details>


### [7] [IntentTwin: Deconfounding Candidate Support and the Oracle-to-Deployment Gap in Offline Web Behavior Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12918)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Daren Zha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出，离线网页行为评测可能因候选集构造方式产生支持集混淆：模型只需识别候选动作是否属于训练支持集，就能命中目标，而不必真正理解行为。通过平衡候选集后，作者更可靠地评估了重排序与目标无关的候选提议能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多离线评测把示范中的正确动作直接放入有限候选集，再衡量模型能否将其排在首位。当目标动作不在训练支持集、而干扰项都来自训练支持集时，目标与干扰项的支持集归属差异便泄露了答案。这会高估模型能力，使离线结果难以反映真实部署表现，因此需要量化并控制候选集的支持组成。

Method: 作者提出支持可识别率，用于衡量候选动作的训练支持集归属能否识别正确答案；并构造支持集平衡的候选集，降低这种线索的影响。研究还评估了一种目标无关的双路径候选提议系统：一路检索训练支持集中的动作，另一路结合开放词汇能力，从网页文档对象模型和语言模型生成候选。实验覆盖筛选后的欧佩拉数据集与心智网页数据集，并对两百个样本进行语义标注。

Result: 在欧佩拉数据集的原始协议中，训练支持集外目标的支持可识别率为一，单靠支持集归属判断即可达到百分之百的首位精确命中率。支持集平衡后，该可识别率降至零。最强条件重排序模型的总体、支持集内及支持集外首位精确命中率分别为百分之七十二点一四、七十九点八八和五十六点六八。目标无关双路径系统的十六候选召回率为百分之六十九点一五，端到端首位精确命中率为百分之五十二点一二；条件重排序的预言机上限为百分之七十四点五零。心智网页数据集上的支持可识别率为百分之二十三点八，预言机与候选提议之间的性能差距为零点二一二五。语义审查显示，支持集外且键值完全匹配的目标中，百分之七十一点五属于语义新颖，百分之二十四点五属于定位符或字符串变体，百分之四属于含糊样本；标注者一致性较高。

Conclusion: 离线评测不能只关注候选集上的排序准确率，还必须控制候选项与训练支持集之间的构成关系。支持集平衡有助于揭示模型对支持集外动作的真实泛化能力；同时，目标无关候选提议与条件重排序之间仍存在明显差距，值得在后续研究和部署评估中重点考察。

Abstract: Offline web-behavior benchmarks frequently insert the demonstrated action into a finite candidate set and then measure reranking. We show that this protocol can create a candidate-support confound: when the target is outside training support while all distractors are sampled from that support, support membership identifies the gold action without modeling behavior. We formalize this effect with support-identifiability rate (SIR), construct support-balanced candidate sets, and evaluate a target-free dual-path proposer that combines support retrieval with open-vocabulary DOM/LM proposals. On filtered OPeRA (4,282/469/992 train/dev/test steps), the original protocol has SIR 1.0000 on target-OOV rows and a support-only rule reaches 1.0000 OOV Exact@1. Support balancing reduces SIR to 0.0000; the strongest conditional reranker obtains 0.7214 overall Exact@1, 0.7988 on IV rows, and 0.5668 on OOV rows. The target-free dual-path system reaches Proposal Recall@16 0.6915 and end-to-end Exact@1 0.5212, compared with oracle conditional Exact@1 0.7450. On Mind2Web, the same audit yields SIR 0.2380 and an oracle-to-proposal gap of 0.2125. A 200-row semantic audit labels 0.7150 of exact-key OOV targets as semantically novel, 0.2450 as locator/string variation, and 0.0400 as ambiguous (Cohen's $κ=0.8173$). These measurements show why support composition must be controlled alongside candidate accuracy when offline results are used to reason about deployment.

</details>


### [8] [Capacity Allocation at the Source: Sparse Target Optimization for LLM Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12995)
*Dongyao Chen,Jiayun Lei,Zhiyuan Ji,Zhiying Deng,Wei Liu,Xiaobo Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出在构造知识编辑目标隐状态时加入稀疏约束，减少无关成分，从而降低不同编辑之间的干扰，提升新旧知识的共存能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑研究主要关注如何修改模型参数并保留其他知识，但目标隐状态的构造同样会影响模型有限容量的分配。冗余的隐状态成分可能使一次编辑波及其他知识。

Method: 作者从理论上建立参数空间干扰与目标隐状态干扰之间的正相关关系，并据此提出基于一范数正则化的方法，剪除每个知识向量中不必要的冗余成分，只保留较关键的部分。

Result: 在多种大型语言模型和基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够有效减少知识编辑间的干扰，并改善编辑效果。

Conclusion: 知识编辑的效果不仅取决于参数修改策略，也取决于目标隐状态的设计。通过稀疏化目标表示，可以减少编辑对其他知识的影响，促进多项编辑结果共存。

Abstract: Knowledge editing has emerged as a promising approach for efficiently updating embedded knowledge in large language models (LLMs). It first computes an ideal target hidden state that steers the LLM toward the new output, and then edits the model parameters so that the original hidden state is mapped to this target. While most existing methods focus on the second stage of parameter editing in preserving other knowledge, we find that the upstream construction of the target hidden state also plays an important role in allocating the model's limited capacity space. We first establish a theoretical connection between interference in the parameter space and interference between target hidden states, showing that the two are positively correlated. Building on this insight, we propose a simple yet effective $\ell_1$ regularization method that prunes away unnecessary ``tentacles'' of each knowledge vector, retaining only the essential components. With fewer extraneous tentacles, each edit affects less other knowledge, enabling better coexistence. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs and benchmark datasets verify the effectiveness of our method. Code: https://github.com/wintersdragon-c/STO.

</details>


### [9] [khammoon: From a Linguistic Guideline to a Thai Morpheme Segmenter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13012)
*Warit Sirichotedumrong,Adisai Na-Thalang,Chanakan Wittayasakpan,Kritsadha Phatcharoen,Kunat Pipatanakul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出以泰语最小有义语素为单位进行切分，减少复合词边界歧义；实验显示该表示不损害下游任务表现，并训练出高精度、轻量级的切分模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泰语书写通常不以空格分隔词语，而现有工具多遵循语料库中的词级规范。由于泰语“词”的定义缺乏统一共识，这类规范难以稳定处理灵活构词与复合词，容易造成任意切分及下游表示歧义。论文因此转向语素层级，寻求更一致、明确的形态表示。

Method: 研究以泰语最小有义单位作为切分目标，将语言学原则整理为正式判定指南，并取得近乎完美的人类标注一致性（卡帕系数约为0.99）。随后利用大语言模型扩展标注，再以人工标签修正系统性错误。最后训练一个约一百万参数的卷积双向长短期记忆网络切分器，以适应高效的中央处理器推理。

Result: 语素级模型在词性标注、命名实体识别和情感分类任务上，整体达到或超过词级基线。所训练的切分器在域外文本上的边界F1值达到0.992。研究同时公开了判定指南、数据集、模型及软件库。

Conclusion: 以语素为基础的泰语切分能够提供更一致的形态表示，消除复合词切分中的部分歧义，并在不牺牲下游任务性能的前提下提升表示质量。研究还表明，结合形式化指南、大规模辅助标注与人工纠错，可以构建高精度且易于部署的切分方案。

Abstract: Thai is written without spaces between words, making segmentation a foundational step in most Thai NLP pipelines. Existing tools primarily reproduce the word-level conventions of corpora such as BEST; however, because the notion of a "word" in Thai lacks a unified linguistic consensus, these conventions struggle with productive compounding, leading to arbitrary boundaries and ambiguity in downstream representations. Instead, we propose operating at the morphological level, focusing on khammoon, the smallest atomic unit that carries meaning. Segmenting at the morphemic level provides an explicit, consistent morphological representation that eliminates compounding ambiguity. Crucially, this representation benefits downstream learning: models trained on morphemes perform on par with or exceed word-level baselines across part-of-speech tagging, named-entity recognition, and sentiment classification, demonstrating that linguistic consistency improves representation quality without downstream performance penalty. To operationalize this representation, we translate linguistic principles into a formal decision guideline with near-perfect human agreement ($κ\approx 0.99$), scale annotation with an LLM, and correct systematic errors with human labels. Finally, we train khammoon, a lightweight 1M-parameter convolutional BiLSTM segmenter designed for efficient CPU inference, achieving a boundary F1 of 0.992 on out-of-domain text. The guideline, datasets, model, and library are all publicly released.

</details>


### [10] [The Indic Token Tax in 2026: Measuring Tokenizer. Disparity and Its Cost for the Languages of India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13058)
*Johin Johny Arimbur,Ankur Ashit Ghosh,Shervin Sam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文比较九种公开分词器处理印度二十五种语言时的分词成本差异，发现非英语文本通常需要更多词元，且分词边界与词素边界的吻合度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业语言模型接口按词元计费。若同一内容在某种语言中被切分为更多词元，该语言的使用成本会更高、处理速度可能更慢，也会在固定上下文窗口中容纳更少文本。论文旨在量化这种语言间的不平等，并检验分词器是否能反映语言的形态结构。

Method: 研究评估九种公开分词器，涵盖多种主流模型及印度本土模型。数据包括印度二十五种语言与英语对齐的专业翻译句子，以及一万三千余组人工翻译的印地语英语社交媒体文本。研究比较各语言相对于英语的词元数量，并以印地语和马拉地语的形态切分数据评估分词边界与词素边界的重合程度。此外，论文依据公开价格和明确假设，估算客服场景中的费用差异。

Result: 在某主流模型的分词器中，同一内容所需词元数约为英语的1.57至5.01倍；使用奥尔奇基文字的桑塔利语可达13.7倍。另一代模型的分词器在部分语言上的差异最高达12.8倍。所测第三方分词器中，只有一组本土模型的分词器能让所有语言的倍数低于2.6，中位数为1.53。形态评估显示，没有分词器能将超过35%的词内部边界放在词素边界上，边界准确度与召回率的综合指标最高为0.48。费用示例估算，在每月处理十万次对话的设定下，英语客服约需五万六千六百四十卢比，马拉地语客服约需十万二千八百二十九卢比；这只是基于假设的估算，并非实际生产成本测量。

Conclusion: 分词器会造成显著的语言相关成本与上下文容量差异，而且现有分词边界并未充分贴合印地语和马拉地语的词素结构。论文提供了量化证据与可复现资源，并指出需要改进分词方法；作者另有配套论文提出一种旨在降低这种语言成本差异的分词器。

Abstract: Commercial language model APIs bill per token, so a tokenizer that needs more tokens for the same content in one language makes that language more expensive, slower, and able to fit less text in a fixed context window. We measure this disparity for nine public tokenizers, including those of GPT-4, GPT-4o, Qwen2.5, DeepSeek-V3, Mistral-Nemo and four Sarvam models, on all 25 languages of India available in FLORES-200 (2,009 professionally translated sentences aligned with English) and on 13,732 human-translated Hinglish-English social media pairs from PHINC. On GPT-4o's tokenizer the same content needs between 1.57 times (Hindi) and 5.01 times (Odia) the tokens of English, and 13.7 times for Santali in Ol Chiki script; GPT-4's tokenizer needs up to 12.8 times. Sarvam-30B/105B is the only third-party tokenizer that stays below 2.6 times on every language (median 1.53). In an illustrative scenario using official list prices accessed on 5 November 2026 and stated assumptions, a support bot handling 100,000 chats a month on GPT-4o would cost about INR 56,640 in English and INR 102,829 in Marathi; these are estimates, not measured production costs. Against an openly licensed Hindi and Marathi morphological segmentation set, no tokenizer places more than 35% of its word-internal boundaries on morpheme boundaries (boundary F1 at most 0.48). In a companion paper we present a tokenizer that reduces this tax. Code, pinned artifacts and raw token counts are available at https://github.com/HavonAI/indic-token-tax.

</details>


### [11] [A Multi-Word Tokenizer for 204 Languages That Needs Fewer Tokens Than GPT-4o's, Including on English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13059)
*Johin Johny,Ankur Ghosh,Shervin Sam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出多语言分词器的第三、第四和第五版，重点降低印度语言及其他语言的分词数量。实验显示，在多种词表规模下，它们对多数语言比GPT-4o分词器更省词元；第五版也改善了代码分词表现。但研究只评估压缩效果，尚未验证对实际模型性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPT-4o的分词器处理印度语言时，所需词元数可达同等英文内容的1.6至14倍，增加了服务成本与延迟。论文旨在训练更适合多语言文本、尤其是印度语言的分词器，并改善代码分词，同时考察词表大小和训练方式对压缩效果的影响。

Method: 研究者从约200种语言的人工书写文本出发训练分词器。第三版采用字节级字节对编码，词表含262,144个词元；其预分词器会将组合附加符号与对应字母保留在一起，并对无空格书写的文字进行分块。第四版在第三版基础上增加多词训练阶段。第五版则将第四版中仅有的Python代码样本替换为采用宽松许可的15种编程语言代码。研究使用FLORES-200开发测试集比较各版本与GPT-4o分词器所需的词元数，并以句子级自助法检验显著性。

Result: 第三版在印度全部25种语言上的词元数都少于GPT-4o，中位数为其0.74倍；在204种语言中的172种更省，但英文多用7%。第四版在与GPT-4o相同的200,019词元词表规模下，英文少用6.1%，在161种语言及全部25种印度语言上更省；同规模第三版英文多用9.4%，仅在133种语言上占优。第四版词表增至262,144时，英文少用9.6%，在182种语言上更省；增至524,288时，在204种语言中的203种更省。收益在英文、印地语和罗曼语族语言中较大；高棉语、缅甸语和藏语等无空格文字获益较少，且损失子词合并。第五版处理代码所需词元数为GPT-4o的1.03至1.22倍，优于第四版的1.11至1.29倍；同时，印度全部25种语言的中位数仍为0.74倍，英文为0.94倍，相关结果均通过句子级自助法检验。

Conclusion: 从人工多语言文本训练分词器，特别是采用多词训练并扩大词表，能够显著降低多数语言的分词数量；补充多种编程语言样本也可改善代码分词。不同文字系统的收益并不均衡，而且更少词元并不等同于更好的模型能力。论文目前证明的是压缩效果，尚未评估使用第四版或第五版训练的模型。

Abstract: Languages of India need 1.6 to 14 times as many tokens as English on GPT-4o's tokenizer for the same content, which makes them more expensive and slower to serve. We train tokenizers from scratch on human-written text in about 200 languages. Havon v3 is a byte-level BPE tokenizer with 262,144 tokens and a pre-tokenizer that keeps combining marks with their letters and chunks scripts written without spaces. On FLORES-200 devtest it needs fewer tokens than GPT-4o's o200k_base on all 25 languages of India (median 0.74 of GPT-4o's count) and on 172 of 204 languages, but 7% more on English. Havon v4 adds a second, multi-word training stage (SuperBPE) to v3. At exactly GPT-4o's vocabulary size of 200,019 tokens, v4 needs 6.1% fewer tokens than GPT-4o on English and fewer on 161 of 204 languages and all 25 languages of India (median 0.74); at the same size, v3 needs 9.4% more on English and wins on 133. With 262,144 tokens, v4 needs 9.6% fewer tokens on English and fewer on 182 of 204 languages, and with 524,288 tokens it needs fewer on 203 of 204. The gains are largest for English, Hindi and the Romance languages; scripts without spaces, such as Khmer, Burmese and Tibetan, gain little and lose subword merges. Havon v5 replaces v4's Python-only code sample with permissively licensed code in 15 languages: on code it needs 1.03 to 1.22 times GPT-4o's tokens, against 1.11 to 1.29 for v4, while keeping all 25 languages of India (median 0.74, all significant under a sentence-level bootstrap) and English (0.94). These are compression results only: we have not yet evaluated a model trained with v4 or v5.

</details>


### [12] [IFRS Compliance Verification: An Open Multi-LLM Benchmark and Content-Conditioned Ensemble Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13595)
*Aghoghomena Akasukpe,Li Yang,Xinyao Zhou,Chen Shen,Miguel Vargas Martin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一套面向国际财务报告准则核查的多阶段框架，结合领域适配文本表征与学习式集成，在模型意见分歧样本上显著优于单个大语言模型和多数投票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核对企业财务披露与国际财务报告准则条文是否一致，需要专家在大量细分条款间逐项交叉比对，过程耗时、成本高且容易出错。尽管大语言模型有望辅助合规审查，但单模型准确率仍不足以满足高风险应用要求；简单集成也可能受到模型系统性偏差影响，导致整体表现低于最强单模型。

Method: 作者构建包含一万九千九百九十八组准则条文与披露文本的数据集，其中一万一千一百六十一组获得多个大语言模型一致标注，另有八千八百三十七组分歧样本由人工标注。方法先以监管申报文本进行掩码语言建模，再通过无监督文本去噪自编码和对比学习适配领域，最后以有监督对比学习对齐文本表征。随后，将冻结的文本向量与三种大语言模型的概率特征输入梯度提升树集成分类器，并使用树结构帕尔森估计优化参数。框架还包含用于检索相关准则条文的流程。

Result: 在模型意见分歧样本上，集成模型的加权调和平均值为百分之八十一点七，比表现最强的单个大语言模型高十点四个百分点，比多数投票高十四点六个百分点，并优于十一种无监督标签聚合基线。对于未见过的准则条文，检索流程在前十五项结果中的召回率为百分之四十五点三。

Conclusion: 研究表明，领域适配的文本表征与利用模型概率特征的学习式集成相结合，有助于缓解模型偏差并提升财务披露合规核查效果。不过，摘要报告的主要分类提升针对模型意见分歧样本，且未见条文检索召回率仍有限，因此结果支持其作为审计辅助工具，而非完全替代专家判断。作者计划公开数据集、模型检查点、标注平台及分类器实现。

Abstract: Verifying corporate financial disclosures against the International Financial Reporting Standards (IFRS) is a core auditing task in which experts cross-reference disclosures with hundreds of fine-grained regulatory paragraphs: a laborious, costly, and error-prone process that motivates growing interest in large language models (LLMs) as compliance assistants. Yet single-model accuracy remains insufficient for high-stakes use, and naive ensembling is vulnerable to systematic per-model biases that can drag aggregate accuracy below that of the strongest individual annotator. This gap is addressed with a multi-stage framework, supported by a new benchmark of 19,998 IFRS regulation-disclosure pairs comprising 11,161 unanimous multi-LLM labels and 8,837 human annotations on items where the LLMs disagreed. The framework couples a three-stage domain-adapted sentence embedding pipeline (BERT masked language modeling on SEC EDGAR filings, unsupervised TSDAE and SimCSE adaptation, and supervised contrastive alignment) with a learned ensemble classifier that fuses frozen embeddings and LLM probability features from GPT-4.1, Claude Sonnet 4, and Gemini 2.5 Pro via Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) optimized gradient-boosted trees. On the disagreement set, the ensemble achieves 81.7% weighted F1, exceeding the strongest single LLM by 10.4 points and majority voting by 14.6 points, and outperforms eleven unsupervised aggregation baselines; the retrieval pipeline attains Recall@15 of 45.3% on unseen regulations. The dataset, model checkpoints, annotation platform, and classifier implementation will be publicly released.

</details>


### [13] [Catch It Where It Happens: Real Time Error Correction During LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13107)
*Rotem Elimelech,Michael Shalyt,Ido Kaminer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需调用额外模型、可实时检查推理步骤的确定性验证方法，并在符号数学任务中提升了大语言模型的答题准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学、科学和工程推理中可能自信地犯错，错误步骤还会引发后续推理偏离。现有方法往往只检查最终答案并要求模型重新作答，或依赖额外验证模型，增加显卡资源占用和响应延迟。

Method: 在模型生成推理内容时，利用代码对可形式化检验的步骤进行在线、确定性验证。若发现错误，立即指出问题，让模型从当前步骤继续，而不必从头生成，也无需调用验证模型或占用额外显卡资源。研究以符号数学为例，使用计算机代数系统快速核验推导中的表达式。

Result: 在符号数学基准测试中，对三种开放权重模型进行评估后，该方法使准确率提高了1.4至5.6个百分点，相当于错误答案净减少1.8%至14.9%；额外词元用量仅增加6%至7%。

Conclusion: 在线确定性验证能够以较低开销减少推理错误，并可与模型能力提升互补。随着更多推理步骤能够由确定性检查器验证，该方法有望惠及不同模型及更广泛的任务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trusted with mathematical, scientific and engineering work, yet their mistakes are often confidently written and hard to spot. A single wrong step tends to propagate, making the model waste inference tokens following a false premise. Existing safeguards either check only the final answer and restart from scratch, or rely on a verifier LLM that competes for GPU capacity and slows down response time. We propose online, deterministic verification: many kinds of reasoning steps can be validated by code, so a step can be verified in real time, as it is written, without any model call or GPU use. Whenever a check fails, we point out the mistake and let the model continue from the same spot. We demonstrate this approach on symbolic mathematics, where derivations already contain explicit expressions that a computer algebra system can check in milliseconds. Tested on the ASyMoB benchmark with three open-weights models, our online validation improves accuracy by 1.4-5.6 percentage points, corresponding to a 1.8-14.9% net reduction in incorrect answers, while increasing token usage by only 6-7%. Because the verifier is independent of the model, the approach complements progress in LLMs rather than competing with it: every new model can benefit immediately, and the benefit grows as deterministic checkers extend to more kinds of reasoning steps.

</details>


### [14] [HiCAP: Hierarchical Cultural Adaptation via Personality for Dialogue Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13207)
*Lingzhi Shen,Xiaohao Cai,Yunfei Long,Imran Razzak,Shoaib Jameel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出融合文化适应与人格建模的对话生成框架，通过分层控制生成风格，在文化语气与个体表达之间进行协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话生成方法对文化适应的关注有限，尤其较少考虑人格特质如何影响文化表达和沟通风格。因此，即使回复符合某种文化规范，也未必能体现不同个体的表达差异。

Method: 该框架采用人格条件控制机制，分层调节回复风格：先使回复与目标文化语气相符，再依据个体人格属性细化语言表达与内容。模型结合监督微调和指令调优，并使用动态指令模板及多智能体示范，以覆盖多样的文化与人格组合。

Result: 在文化人格对话数据集和中文人格化情感对话数据集上的实验显示，该框架在多项对话生成指标上取得了整体最佳表现，表明其能够较好地适应文化差异并刻画人格特征。摘要未提供具体指标、数值或显著性检验结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将文化规范与人格特质纳入分层生成控制，有助于提升对话系统的文化适应性和个体化表达能力。其结论主要受到摘要所述数据集与评价指标范围的支持。

Abstract: Cultural adaptation in dialogue systems refers to the ability to generate responses that conform to linguistic norms and social expectations across different cultures. However, existing dialogue generation approaches pay limited attention to the role of personality in shaping cultural expression and communication style. We therefore propose HiCAP, a novel framework that integrates personality traits into culturally adaptive dialogue generation. HiCAP employs a personality-conditioned control mechanism that hierarchically modulates response style by first aligning it with the cultural tone and then refining linguistic expression and content according to individual personality attributes. To embed these factors, the model combines supervised fine-tuning and instruction-based tuning, supported by dynamic instruction templates and multi-agent demonstrations to capture diverse combinations of culture and personality. Experiments on CulturalPersonas and CPED show that HiCAP achieves the strongest overall performance across various dialogue generation metrics, demonstrating effective cultural adaptability and personality modelling.

</details>


### [15] [StaRSE: Compact Russian Sentence Embeddings with a Sign-Coded Static Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14236)
*Boris Malashenko,Ivan Jarsky,Egor Soldatov,Vladislav Luzhnov,Sviatoslav Stumpf,Vladislav Shmatkov,Viacheslav Shalamov,Andrey Stankevich,Valeria Efimova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种紧凑型俄语静态句子编码器，以符号位和词向量范数压缩词表示，并通过平均池化生成句向量；在俄语基准上取得较强效果，同时显著降低模型体积与中央处理器推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于变换器的句子编码器检索效果良好，但在中央处理器上运行或需要频繁更新嵌入时，推理成本较高。本文旨在移除部署阶段的变换器推理，同时尽量维持检索质量，并减少模型占用和编码延迟。

Method: 作者提出紧凑型俄语静态句子编码器：将词向量方向存为符号位，另行保存词向量范数；编码句子时，对按范数缩放的符号方向进行平均池化。模型以俄语基础双向编码器初始化，使用俄语相邻句和面向具体任务的句对进行微调，随后通过对比学习阶段直接优化经过符号投影的词表。实验还通过消融分析考察相邻句监督、词向量范数以及直接符号投影后训练的作用。

Result: 在俄语多任务嵌入基准上，模型的任务平均分为51.16，比纳入比较的最强静态基线高7.98分。模型封装后占用11.3兆字节，单句中央处理器平均延迟为0.08毫秒；与质量最接近的上下文编码器相比，后者估算的16位参数占用为55.7兆字节，平均延迟为1.43毫秒。

Conclusion: 摘要表明，通过符号位压缩、单独保存范数及针对符号投影表示的后训练，可以构建高效的俄语静态句子编码器，在保持有竞争力的检索表现的同时，大幅降低模型体积和中央处理器推理延迟。

Abstract: Transformer sentence encoders provide strong retrieval quality, but their inference cost becomes a bottleneck when embeddings must be produced on CPUs or refreshed frequently. We introduce StaRSE, a compact Russian static sentence encoder that removes Transformer inference at deployment time: token directions are stored as sign bits, token norms are stored separately, and sentences are encoded by mean pooling over norm-scaled sign directions. StaRSE is initialized from ruBERT-base, fine-tuned on Russian adjacent-sentence and task-shaped pairs, and post-trained with a contrastive stage that directly optimizes the sign-projected token table. On RuMTEB, StaRSE reaches a task mean of $51.16$, $7.98$ points above the strongest included static baseline. The packaged model occupies $11.3$ MiB and has a mean CPU latency of $0.08$ ms per sentence, compared with an estimated $55.7$ MiB 16-bit parameter footprint and $1.43$ ms mean CPU latency for the closest-quality contextual encoder. Ablations examine adjacent-sentence supervision, token norms, and direct sign-projected post-training.

</details>


### [16] [Deletion Sensitivity Is a Property of the Model: A Three-Condition Measurement across Seven Open Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13223)
*Hanzhang Jia,Liheng Zeng,Zhiyu Yang,Yi Gao,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究多轮工具调用中推理历史被删除的影响，发现影响因模型而异，且可通过简短测试测量，应纳入模型路由与结果汇总。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮工具会话中的推理模型会在每轮生成推理轨迹，后续轮次依赖这段历史。但当请求跨不同服务提供方转发时，历史可能被删除，而这种处理带来的任务成本此前缺乏直接测量。论文旨在量化这一影响，避免将其视为不可见的默认代价。

Method: 研究对 794 个任务进行盲测，涵盖 7 个开放权重推理模型、3 个随机种子和 3 种呈现条件，并使用官方检查器评估表现。实验直接比较多轮工具调用中完整保留与删除推理历史的情况；另对一个模型将删除比例从 0% 调至 100%，检验测量结果的稳定性。

Result: 删除推理历史造成的表现变化因模型而异，测得范围为 -4.4 至 +51.3 个百分点，范围两端均排除零；因此，删除历史并非对所有模型都产生相同方向或幅度的影响。对一个模型进行不同删除比例的复测时，测量读数最多变化 1.5 个百分点。研究还发现，删除敏感性是模型层面的属性：同一模型在不同部署中的读数保持稳定；重新训练或重新发布的模型则应视为新模型重新测量。

Conclusion: 推理历史删除的影响可以作为模型属性进行测量，而不应被当作固定且隐形的系统成本。作者建议将该测量结果作为先验信息纳入模型路由与结果汇总，并指出只需对模型分别进行一次保留历史测试和一次删除历史测试，即可完成成本较低的初筛。

Abstract: Reasoning models write a reasoning trace at every turn of a multi-turn tool session, and later turns read this history. Across 94k ShareGPT conversations, 73 percent of user interactions span multiple turns. Upstream providers bind each trace to its issuer, so a request forwarded across issuers arrives with its history deleted, and the task cost of this stripping remains unmeasured. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first direct, blind, multi-seed measurement of full reasoning-history deletion in multi-turn tool calling, through three rendering conditions, seven open-weight reasoning models, three seeds, 794 tasks, and the official checker. The same deletion spans -4.4 to +51.3 points with both ends excluding zero, and for the one model re-dosed from 0 to 100 percent deletion, the reading varies by at most 1.5 points. Deletion sensitivity is a model-level property. The reading follows the model itself and stays constant across deployments, and a retrained or re-released model is a new model that a two-cell screen, one retention run and one deletion run, re-reads for the cost of two runs. Deletion sensitivity is thus a measurable property of the model, and the reading must enter routing and aggregation as prior information rather than persist as an invisible default cost.

</details>


### [17] [Point-in-Time Encoders for Earnings Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14325)
*Alessio Brini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建严格遵守时间边界的财报电话会议文本编码器，发现其显著改善检索效果，并提升部分预测任务表现；但对收益率预测没有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文本检索器常用全部现有文本训练，但回测时若编码器读过预测日期之后的材料，就会产生前视偏差。研究旨在构建符合历史信息可得性的编码器，并检验其对检索和预测的价值。

Method: 研究使用412,248场财报电话会议，按2010至2024年的年度截止日期训练多个模型版本，每个版本仅接触截止日期及之前的文本。模型采用四种预训练方案，并以电话会议中的问答对进行微调。部分模型规模为3,380万参数；另有模型以先前版本为基础持续训练。研究评估其在留出电话会议上的答案检索效果，以及对次日收益率和盈利意外预测的影响。

Result: 仅用电话会议训练的编码器，在所有测试季度的留出会议检索任务中均优于所测试的通用检索器。其文本向量还使基于历史收益率和公司规模的次日收益率预测与实际收益的秩相关性提高约一半。持续训练得到的较新版本，在较早季度的检索效果优于当时可用的版本；使用其检索段落作答的语言模型也获得相应提升。2024年版本对盈利意外的预测较好，但在未被任何版本读过的电话会议上，提升的不确定区间包含零；收益率预测则没有改善。

Conclusion: 严格控制训练文本的时间范围，可以有效缓解回测中的前视偏差，并带来显著的检索收益；较新的版本也可能改善基于检索材料的问答和盈利意外预测。不过，这些收益并未扩展到收益率预测，说明时间点一致的文本编码器主要适用于检索相关任务，其预测价值取决于具体目标。

Abstract: Text encoders turn earnings calls into vectors for search, for language models that answer from retrieved passages, and for forecasting models. A live retriever can use an encoder trained once on all available text, as finance retrievers are, but a backtest needs an encoder that read no text written after the date of each vector. To address this look-ahead bias, from 412,248 calls we train vintages under four pretraining recipes, with yearly cutoffs from 2010 to 2024, and each vintage reads only text dated on or before its cutoff. We then fine-tune each vintage on the question-answer pairs of the calls themselves. The vintages we train on calls alone, with 33.8M parameters, retrieve answers on held-out calls better than the general retrievers we tested in every test quarter. Their vectors also raise by about half the rank correlation between next-day returns and forecasts built from past returns and firm size. When each vintage continues the previous one, a later vintage retrieves better on earlier quarters than the vintage available at the time, and a point-in-time language model that answers from the passages the later vintage retrieves inherits the gain. The 2024 vintage of our families also forecasts earnings surprises better, although the interval of this gain includes zero on calls that no vintage read. It forecasts returns no better, so point-in-time vintages matter most for retrieval and little for forecasts of returns.

</details>


### [18] [What drives the emergence of symbolic mechanisms in large language models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13270)
*Melody Zixuan Li,Taylor Webb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，训练数据的多样性比网络架构更能推动神经网络形成支持抽象推理的符号机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现，大型语言模型中会涌现支持抽象推理的符号机制，但这些机制由何种因素驱动尚不清楚。本文旨在区分数据分布与架构归纳偏置的作用。

Method: 研究者从零开始训练神经网络，使其完成抽象序列任务，并分别改变网络架构和数据分布特征。随后通过表征分析、注意力分析和因果中介分析，考察符号机制是否出现及其强弱；并在实体—属性绑定任务中检验结果。

Result: 从零训练的变换器模型确实形成了符号机制。沿多个维度提高数据多样性，会增强符号机制的特征。相似特征也出现在改造后的变换器架构乃至多层感知机中，表明特定架构并非形成这些机制的必要条件；实体—属性绑定任务中也观察到一致趋势。

Conclusion: 研究将数据多样性识别为神经网络中涌现符号计算的主要驱动因素，而非特定网络架构。结果提示，丰富多样的训练分布可能比架构设计更关键地促进抽象、可组合的计算机制形成。

Abstract: Recent work has identified a set of emergent symbolic mechanisms that support abstract reasoning in large language models, but it remains unclear what factors drive the emergence of these mechanisms. To address this question, we trained neural networks from scratch on abstract sequence tasks, varying both architectural and data distributional factors, and investigated the effects of these factors on the emergence of symbolic mechanisms. Using representational, attentional, and causal mediation analyses, we first confirmed that transformer-based language models trained from scratch on our task developed symbolic mechanisms. We then found that, across multiple axes of data diversity, greater diversity produced stronger symbolic signatures. Surprisingly, the same signatures emerged in modified transformer architectures and even multilayer perceptrons, indicating that architectural inductive biases are not necessary for the development of symbolic mechanisms. We also observed consistent patterns in an entity-attribute binding task. Together, these findings identify data diversity, not architecture, as the primary driver of emergent symbolic computation in neural networks.

</details>


### [19] [Measuring Definition Sensitivity in LLM Annotation of Social Science Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13339)
*Bogdan Mamaev,Qizhou Wang,Christopher Leckie,Matteo Vergani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，大语言模型的文本标注会受到提示中概念定义的影响，因此标注结果应与所用定义及定义敏感性一并报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学文本标注通常依赖成本高、难以规模化的人工流程。虽然研究者开始使用大语言模型进行标注，但仇恨言论、假新闻和恐怖主义等概念往往存在多个理论上合理的定义；此外，尚不清楚模型是否真正依据提示中的定义作出判断，还是依赖预训练中形成的概念理解。

Method: 研究者让模型对同一批文本分别使用两种相互竞争的概念定义进行标注，并以两组标注之间的分歧衡量“定义敏感性”。作为对照，他们在不改变定义要求的前提下改写其中一种定义，并以两种表述产生的标注分歧衡量“释义基线”。研究覆盖六个概念和三个大语言模型。

Result: 在六个概念和三个模型中，替换为竞争性定义所引发的平均标注变化始终多于单纯改写定义表述所引发的变化。这表明模型的标注确实会受到提示定义的影响，而不只是由措辞变化造成。

Conclusion: 大语言模型生成的标注应结合产生这些标注的概念定义来解释。相关研究还应报告标注者对定义的敏感程度，以及由定义改写造成的释义基线，以便区分概念定义差异与措辞差异的影响。

Abstract: In social science research, text annotation is a fundamental task that is traditionally a costly, hard-to-scale manual process. Researchers have used large language models (LLMs) to perform annotation by writing a definition of a concept into a prompt - a rule that determines whether a label should be attributed to a text depending on its content. However, concepts such as hate speech, fake news, and terrorism typically have several theoretically defensible competing definitions. It is also unknown whether the model actually applied the definition to provide the label or the label was based on the model's pretrained notion of the concept. To test this, we have the model annotate the same texts twice, each time with a different definition. We call the disagreement between the two sets of annotations definition sensitivity. As a control, we paraphrase one definition but leave its requirements unchanged - the disagreement between the annotations with the two wordings becomes the paraphrase floor. Across six concepts and three LLMs, a swap of competing definitions always changes more annotations, on average, than a paraphrase. This shows that the annotations depend on the prompted definition, so any set of annotations should be interpreted together with the definition that produced it. The LLM annotator's definition sensitivity and its paraphrase floor should both be reported in such work.

</details>


### [20] [Understanding Students' Rationales for AI-Mediated Cheating: An Instructor Perspective from Online Forums Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13358)
*Iman Mohammadi,Sina Rismanchian,Ali Keramati,Shayan Salehi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究分析教师论坛上的二百零一万条帖子，归纳教师认为学生使用人工智能作弊的四类动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能广泛应用加剧了学术诚信危机。既有研究多关注作弊现象及其规模，较少探讨教师如何理解学生以人工智能实施违规行为的理由。

Method: 研究对教师网络论坛上的二百零一万条帖子进行分析，并采用定性分析归纳教师对学生使用人工智能作弊理由的看法。

Result: 分析归纳出四类主要主题：利用学术制度漏洞、数字与学术素养差距、表现焦虑，以及不诚实行为的常态化。

Conclusion: 教师认为，学生借助人工智能作弊并非由单一因素驱动，而与学术制度、能力差距、压力及对不诚实行为的接受程度有关。研究揭示的是教师的认知与解释，并不直接证明这些因素就是学生作弊的实际动机。

Abstract: The extensive adoption of AI has precipitated a crisis in academic integrity. While studies attempt to quantify cheating, less is known about the motivations instructors believe students have for using AI in prohibited ways. We analyze 2.01 million posts on r/Professors to understand how instructors perceive students' justifications for AI-mediated cheating. Our qualitative analyses reveal four primary themes: (1) Academic System Gaming, (2) Digital-Academic Literacy Gap, (3) Performance Anxiety, and (4) Normalization of Dishonesty.

</details>


### [21] [Mapping Instructors' Attitudes Towards Cheating with AI: An Exploratory Analysis Using Forum Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13366)
*Iman Mohammadi,Ali Keramati,Sina Rismanchian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究分析大型语言模型出现后大学教师关于作弊的网络讨论，梳理教师反对学术作弊的动机与应对策略，并探讨人工智能融入教育的注意事项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式人工智能能够完成部分学生作业，使对话式作弊与学术诚信问题更加突出。研究旨在借助教师的实际讨论，了解他们如何看待学术诚信、为何反对作弊，以及人工智能给教学带来的新挑战。

Method: 研究收集并分析了聊天生成式人工智能发布后，大学教师论坛中的约二百零一万条帖子与评论，内容来自有关作弊讨论的主题帖及回复；据此归纳教师劝阻作弊的理由和采用的策略。

Result: 研究综合了教师应对作弊的动机与做法，并据此指出人工智能与教育结合时需要考虑的问题。摘要未提供具体动机类别、策略类型或定量分析结果，因此无法进一步确认各项发现的细节。

Conclusion: 教师论坛讨论可为理解生成式人工智能背景下的学术诚信观念提供大规模、贴近实践的材料。研究强调，教育机构在引入人工智能时，应同时审视作弊防范、学习过程、个人知识发展与公平等方面的影响。

Abstract: In teaching and learning environments, cheating and the violation of academic integrity have always been one of the challenges, involving different levels of concerns such as administrative, personal knowledge, learning, and equity. The introduction of large language models has brought cheating in dialogue to the forefront, given the apparent capabilities of these models to complete students' academic assignments. This study aims to leverage recent dialogues among university professors about cheating with Generative AI to better understand their beliefs about academic honesty. In doing so, we analyze a corpus of 2.01 million posts (threads and comments) in the r/Professors subreddit discussing cheating after the release of ChatGPT, providing a better understanding of teachers' motivations to discourage cheating and the strategies they use to do so. We synthesize the why and how of addressing cheating to identify caveats about the future of AI and academic integration.

</details>


### [22] [BioMem: Agentic Memory for Biomedical LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13426)
*Mingchen Li,Yongkang Xiao,Rumeng Li,Rui Zhang,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究系统评估生物医学领域的智能体记忆，并提出免训练的混合记忆检索与利用框架，在问答和对话记忆任务上提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在生物医学应用中展现出潜力，智能体记忆也有望支持跨任务、跨交互的信息保存与使用。然而，记忆系统能否可靠地保留、检索、更新并利用生物医学信息，仍缺乏系统评估。研究重点关注任务效果、检索质量、信息保真度和长期鲁棒性。

Method: 研究在三类场景中评估五种记忆方法与四种大型语言模型：生物医学知识推理、临床预测和长期对话记忆，并分析记忆构建方式、证据相关性及对话历史对下游表现的影响。在此基础上，提出免训练的混合记忆检索与利用框架，联合提升证据的可获取性及其面向任务的利用能力。

Result: 该框架在生物医学问答和对话记忆基准上平均相对提升百分之八点六，并在部分临床预测任务中取得额外收益。它使对话回答错误减少百分之二十四点九，并弥合了相较于完整上下文推理时百分之四十八的性能差距。

Conclusion: 研究系统揭示了生物医学智能体记忆在相关信息检索、信息保真和推理利用方面的挑战，并表明兼顾证据检索与任务适配利用的免训练方法能够提升记忆增强型语言模型的效果与可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated considerable potential in biomedical applications, while agentic memory systems have emerged as a promising approach to preserving and utilizing information across tasks and interactions. However, a fundamental question remains: how effectively and reliably do these systems retain, retrieve, update, and utilize biomedical information? In this study, we systematically investigate agentic memory in the biomedical domain, focusing on task effectiveness, retrieval quality, information fidelity, and long-term robustness. We evaluate five memory approaches across four LLMs in three representative settings: biomedical knowledge reasoning, clinical prediction, and long-term conversational memory. Our analyses reveal how memory construction, evidence relevance, and conversational history shape downstream biomedical performance, highlighting the distinct challenges of retrieving relevant information, preserving its fidelity, and effectively utilizing it for reasoning. Building on these findings, we introduce Hybrid Memory Retrieval and Utilization (HMRU), a training-free framework that jointly enhances evidence accessibility and task-aware utilization to improve biomedical reasoning and long-term conversational memory. HMRU achieves an average relative improvement of 8.6% across biomedical question-answering and conversational memory benchmarks, with additional gains in selected clinical prediction tasks. It further reduces conversational answer errors by 24.9% and closes 48.0% of the performance gap to full-context inference. Our findings provide a systematic characterization of biomedical agentic memory and practical insights into developing more effective and reliable memory-augmented LLM systems.

</details>


### [23] [Inherited Weights: Measuring the Tokenization Premium and the Sovereignty Cost of Language Models for the Global South](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13441)
*Paulo Camara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出，模型微调难以改变其基础模型继承的关键属性，并提出更公平的多语言分词评估、无需访问模型权重的主权审计，以及面向能耗与责任约束的模型评价目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国和中国以外部署的许多语言模型，都是少数预训练模型家族的微调版本。微调通常无法根本改变基础模型的词表、知识、文化倾向、记忆数据与许可条件，因此仅评估微调后的模型不足以判断其是否适合特定语言与地区。论文也认为，在能源和责任约束下，单纯追求准确率并不合适。

Method: 研究包含三部分：一是使用《世界人权宣言》二十七篇文章，比较二〇二三至二〇二四年发布的七种分词器在三十一种语言上的分词成本，并明确校正未知词元合并造成的测量偏差；二是提出八项黑箱主权审计测试，供无法访问模型权重的采购方使用；三是主张以每单位能耗的准确性、校准能力和选择性风险作为设计目标，并要求指标不能因模型弃答而虚增。论文还报告了一种专有架构的初步内部测量，并说明其局限。

Result: 旧版分词器对阿姆哈拉语的分词成本溢价最高达十倍；在最新的商业分词器中仍达六点八倍。全球分词器对南美原住民语言造成一点六至二点七倍溢价。以葡萄牙语构建的词表，在受测的十三种使用拉丁字母的全球南方语言中，有十一种的溢价低于所有受测全球分词器；瓜拉尼语和克丘亚语的溢价约降低百分之二十四。论文同时提供数据与测量代码，但摘要未给出八项审计或专有架构的具体结果。

Conclusion: 模型的基础来源会持续影响语言公平性、文化适配、数据与许可等关键性质，不能假设微调足以消除这些限制。论文倡议以可复现的多语言测量和黑箱审计检验模型主权，并在现实资源与责任约束下，将能效、校准和审慎弃答纳入核心评价标准。

Abstract: Most language models deployed outside the United States and China are fine-tuned descendants of a small number of pretrained families. We argue that fine-tuning leaves the decisive properties of a model inherited from its base: vocabulary, knowledge, cultural alignment, memorized data and license. We contribute (i) an updated, reproducible measurement of the tokenization premium across 31 languages and 7 tokenizers released in 2023-2024, on 27 articles of the Universal Declaration of Human Rights, with explicit correction for unknown-token collapse. Premiums reach 10x for Amharic in older tokenizers and remain at 6.8x in the newest commercial one; Indigenous South American languages pay between 1.6x and 2.7x under global tokenizers. A vocabulary built from Portuguese lowers the premium for 11 of 13 Latin-script Global South languages relative to every global tokenizer tested, by about 24% for Guarani and Quechua. (ii) An eight-test black-box sovereignty audit that a buyer can run without access to weights. (iii) A regime argument: under energy and liability constraints, accuracy per joule, calibration and selective risk should replace raw accuracy as design objectives, with metrics that abstention cannot inflate. We close with preliminary internal measurements from a proprietary architecture built around these objectives, reported with their limitations. Data and measurement code are included.

</details>


### [24] [Mobile AI Factory: Building Specialized Language Models for Your Mobile Device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13509)
*Evan Li,Krishna Deshpande,Wen Cheng,Shyamnath Gollakota*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 移动人工智能工厂可针对具体任务和手机，自动构建经过微调与量化的小型语言模型；在多项任务上取得有竞争力的效果，并显著减少搜索所需的计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在手机上运行小型语言模型有助于保护用户隐私，也能在无网络时工作，但模型通常需要针对任务微调、针对设备调整规模。现有流程依赖专家反复调试，且仅凭硬件规格难以准确判断模型在实际手机上的表现，因此需要自动化、设备实测驱动的模型构建方案。

Method: 提出“移动人工智能工厂”，以任务说明和目标手机为输入，由协调语言模型决定调整超参数、挖掘真实数据或生成合成样例，并通过固定规则回退会降低准确率的改动。系统从小到大搜索不同基础模型与量化方案，动态调整准确率目标；同时在目标手机上实测不同推理引擎和后端的表现，以选择适配配置。

Result: 在十项任务和四款安卓手机上的评测中，系统生成的端侧模型在未见测试集上有五项任务超过两个采用五样本提示的前沿模型，最高优势为零点三四；另有三项表现持平。在实体抽取任务中，其搜索结果与穷举训练二十七种模型配置的差距不超过零点零三，而图形处理器耗时减少十九倍。不同手机的最佳配置并不相同；不进行手机实测时，即使提供完整硬件与模型规格，外部模型在简单及中等难度案例中有四分之一、在困难案例中有五分之三未能满足应用要求。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合任务驱动的自动搜索、数据适配和目标设备实测，可以高效构建适合特定手机的小型语言模型。设备上的真实测量对满足应用要求至关重要，单靠规格推断并不可靠。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) running on mobile devices can keep user data private and work without connectivity. But they perform well only when fine-tuned for the target task and sized for the target device, a process that today requires repeated expert tuning. We present {\it Mobile AI Factory}, an agentic system that builds a fine-tuned, quantized SLM for a given task and phone. Given a task specification and a target phone, an orchestrator LLM chooses whether to tune hyperparameters, mine real data, or generate synthetic examples, while fixed rules roll back changes that hurt accuracy. The factory searches from small to large models across base models and quantizations, adapts its accuracy goal as it searches, and uses real measurements of the target phone across inference engines and backends. We evaluate Mobile AI Factory on ten tasks and four Android phones. On an unseen testset, its on-device models outperform two five-shot frontier models on five tasks, by up to 0.34, and match them on three more. Its search comes within 0.03 of exhaustively training all 27 model variants on an entity extraction task, while using 19$\times$ less GPU time. Phone measurements also matter: the best configuration changes across phones. Without phone measurements, Claude Opus 5.5, given the full hardware and model specifications, misses the app's requirements in 25\% of easy and moderate cases and in 60\% of hard ones. Code is available at: https://github.com/evanly-gh/SLM_Factory.

</details>


### [25] [Framing-Induced Vulnerability in Large Language Models: Evidence from Chinese News Headlines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13531)
*Mo Zhou,Yiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，中文大语言模型仅凭标题判断新闻时，会受到标题框架影响；提供正文可显著减轻这种偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益承担新闻评估、分流和审核职责，标题判断因而可能影响信息传播与治理。研究关注模型依据的是新闻事实本身，还是事实的表达方式，并检验仅凭标题进行判断是否可靠。

Method: 研究结合对六万三千九百二十六条中文新闻标题的生态审计与受控实验。实验涵盖三十个真实事件、三十个合成事件和二十九组自然标题配对，由四种中文模型进行评估；研究通过改变标题框架、提供或不提供正文，比较误导性判断与事件严重程度评分，并以小规模人工研究作参照。

Result: 仅改变标题框架，就会改变模型对误导性和事件严重程度的判断，且四种模型的变化方向与排序一致。情绪强化标题使严重程度评分提高零点六五至零点八八分；正向重构标题则使评分降低零点三四至零点五二分。提供正文后，严重程度偏差减少百分之五十四至八十，四种模型的偏差降幅均达到统计显著；人工参照研究也呈现相同方向的效应。

Conclusion: 仅凭标题评估新闻存在可测量的稳健性缺口，模型判断会受到框架而非事实内容的影响。部署与治理评估应将框架敏感性纳入可靠性检查，并尽可能结合新闻正文，而非只依据标题作出判断。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly evaluate, route, and moderate news, so a model's judgment of a headline has become a gatekeeping decision. We ask whether that judgment follows the fact being reported or the way the fact is framed. We combine an ecological audit of 63,926 Chinese news headlines with a controlled experiment. The experiment covers 30 real events, 30 synthetic events, and 29 natural headline pairs, tested on four Chinese models. Changing only the headline frame shifted both perceived misleadingness and event severity, and the direction and ordering of the shifts were the same for DeepSeek, MiMo, Dots, and LongCat. Emotionally intensified headlines raised severity ratings by 0.65 to 0.88 points, whereas positively reframed headlines lowered them by 0.34 to 0.52 points. Supplying the article body reduced this severity bias by 54 to 80 percent, and all four reductions were significant at p < .01. A small human reference study showed the same direction of effect. Headline-only LLM evaluation therefore carries a measurable robustness gap, and framing sensitivity belongs among the reliability properties that deployment and governance check.

</details>


### [26] [How Post-Training Influences Verbalized Algorithmic Computation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13552)
*Niall Dalton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究后训练如何影响模型内部信息与文字化推理的作用，发现强化学习验证奖励阶段生成的推理链更有助于准确解题，但模型对相关推理内容更敏感并不必然带来性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练能够提升语言模型的推理表现，但它究竟如何改变模型对内部信息的利用，以及对文字化思维链的依赖，尚不清楚。作者以多位数加法中的进位值作为已知中间状态，系统比较不同后训练阶段的变化。

Method: 研究对象为经过监督微调、直接偏好优化和强化学习验证奖励训练的奥尔莫三代七十亿参数指令模型检查点。作者使用探针从内部激活中读取进位信息，并通过操纵该信息检验其对输出的控制作用；同时比较不同阶段生成的思维链在各接收检查点上的解题效果，并评估模型对进位相关陈述与匹配随机对照陈述的敏感度。

Result: 探针在监督微调与强化学习验证奖励阶段都能以相近准确率读出进位信息，但操纵该信息无法稳定控制模型输出。相比监督微调或直接偏好优化阶段生成的思维链，强化学习验证奖励阶段生成的思维链在不同接收检查点上都带来更高准确率。强化学习验证奖励后，进位相关陈述的重要性评分高于随机对照；直接偏好优化也表现出类似敏感度，却没有带来相当的准确率提升。

Conclusion: 后训练会实质性改变文字化推理的效用，以及模型对推理内容的敏感程度。更强的内容敏感度并不必然转化为更好的解题表现；因此，评估模型推理变化时，应区分内部信息是否可被读取、推理内容是否影响模型，以及这种影响是否改善任务表现。

Abstract: Post-training improves language-model reasoning, but how it changes the use of internal information and verbalized chain of thought (CoT) remains unclear. We study successive SFT, DPO, and RLVR checkpoints of Olmo-3-7B-Instruct on multi-digit addition, using carry values as known intermediate state. Probes recover carry information from internal activations with similar accuracy in SFT and RLVR, although manipulating this information does not reliably control model outputs. In contrast, the usefulness of verbalized reasoning differs: CoTs produced by RLVR yield higher accuracy across receiving checkpoints than those produced by SFT or DPO. We also show that carry-related statements receive higher importance scores after RLVR than matched random controls. DPO shows similar sensitivity to carry-related statements but without comparable accuracy gains. These results suggest that post-training changes the usefulness of written reasoning and sensitivity to its content in meaningful ways, although greater sensitivity does not necessarily coincide with better problem performance.

</details>


### [27] [Are Dense Rewards Worth Their Tokens in GRPO?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13560)
*Vikash Singh,Weicong Chen,Debargha Ganguly,Yanyan Zhang,Mohsen Hariri,Sreehari Sankar,Wang Yang,Chaoda Song,Alexander Nemecek,Osama Zafar,Yuting Shao,Kai Ye,Biyao Zhang,Xinpeng Li,Shouren Wang,Erman Ayday,Jing Ma,Sanmukh Rao Kuppannagari,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究显示，只有在生成令牌数和训练时间相同的条件下比较训练方法，才能可靠判断其效果；若采用这一公平比较，若干看似有效的密集奖励方法反而不如标准组相对策略优化，而挑选难度适中的提示词可带来小幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可依据最终答案是否正确来训练语言模型推理。常用的组相对策略优化会把相同功劳分给解答中的所有令牌，因此研究者提出密集过程奖励，为中间步骤分配奖励。但这类奖励常需额外生成续写，计算成本可能很高；只按训练更新次数比较，或仅在单批数据上进行梯度检验，都没有充分计入这笔成本。本文旨在检验：在计算预算一致时，这些训练干预是否仍然有效。

Method: 作者提出匹配计算量的评估方案，要求各训练干预使用相同的生成令牌预算和训练图形处理器时数，并在预先登记的实验中控制可能的副作用。研究共进行一千六百六十八次实验，覆盖六种模型；评估多种密集奖励方法、逐令牌梯度检验，以及按模型预测成功概率挑选提示词的策略，并通过新随机种子和未见过的问题检验可重复性。

Result: 在一千五百亿参数以下的相关模型实验中，蒙特卡洛奖励按训练更新次数比较时优于标准方法，但在计算量匹配后，最终准确率反而低十四点三个百分点；按训练时间匹配时低七点八个百分点。四种密集奖励变体即使获得相同训练时间，也都未能超过标准方法。逐令牌梯度检验能正确排列奖励方法的相对表现，却将效果高估一至两个百分点，误差可与待检测的提升相当。相较之下，训练预测成功概率最接近二分之一的提示词，在两个模型上分别提升一点七和零点八个百分点；提升在新随机种子和未见问题上重现。简单规则——候选提示词被预测为失败的概率高于成功概率——在六项预先登记测试中的五项准确预测了效果出现的位置。上述收益主要发生在训练早期；将标准方法的初始学习率加倍后，结果与这些收益相差不到半个百分点。

Conclusion: 训练干预必须在生成令牌数和训练时间等计算预算匹配的条件下验证，否则额外计算可能造成虚假的性能优势。该评估方式推翻了部分密集奖励方法的表面收益，同时确认了提示词筛选带来的有限、可复现提升；不过，这种提升与提高标准方法的初始学习率所取得的效果接近。

Abstract: RLVR trains language models to reason by rewarding correct final answers. A widely used algorithm, group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), gives every token of a solution the same credit, so dense process rewards have been proposed to credit intermediate steps, for example with a Monte-Carlo reward that scores each step by sampling continuations from it. Comparing such a reward with GRPO per training update, or vetting it with a gradient test on a single batch, leaves out what it costs: in our setting, the continuations alone take 91% of all generated tokens. We introduce a matched-compute protocol that gives every training intervention the same budget, measured in generated tokens and training GPU-hours, and tests it in preregistered studies with controls for side effects. Across 1,668 runs on six models, matching compute reverses the ranking. On Qwen2.5-1.5B, the Monte-Carlo reward leads standard GRPO per update, yet ends 14.3 percentage points lower at equal tokens and 7.8 points lower at equal training time, and none of four dense-reward variants outperforms GRPO even with as much training time. A per-token gradient test orders these rewards as training does but overestimates each by one to two points, as much as the gains it is meant to detect. In contrast, training on the prompts whose predicted success is nearest 1/2 improves accuracy at matched compute, by 1.7 points on Qwen3-0.6B and 0.8 on Qwen2.5-1.5B. These gains replicate on fresh seeds and unseen problems, and a simple rule, that the candidate prompts are predicted to fail more often than not, predicted where they appear in five of six preregistered tests. Both gains come early in training, and standard GRPO with twice the initial learning rate comes within half a point of them. We argue that training interventions should be validated at matched compute, which overturns some apparent gains and confirms others.

</details>


### [28] [An evidence-traceable benchmark of clinical reasoning in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13562)
*Zhiqun Zhao,Xiaodong Yang,Bruce Gu,Ming Li,Yi Fu,Song Li,Qi Mei,Zhi Liu,Kai Zhang,Kexue Fu,Jing Wu,Xiaofei Lu,Yule Li,Zheng Wang,Haiquan Lu,Xiaoming Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究构建了可追溯至临床证据原文的医学大语言模型基准，发现模型对指南知识的表现远好于其在真实场景中的证据匹配与建议制定；基准成绩不等同于临床有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学大语言模型基准分数若无法追溯到临床证据，就难以判断模型回答是否有依据，也难以解释其实际临床能力。研究旨在建立证据可核查的评估方式，并检验模型能否将医学知识应用于具体患者情境。

Method: 研究团队构建医学证据基准，包含一千五百三十六道由医师编制的题目，覆盖指南知识、标准化临床情境和真实世界检验情境三个层级。每项医学主张对应的评分标准均链接至带版本信息的来源文本片段。研究评估了十种大语言模型，并在一千二百九十六项评分标准层面的判断中，将自动评分与三名医师的多数意见进行比较。

Result: 证据追溯得分在指南知识层级为零点九七五至零点九九七，在标准化情境中为零点二九二至零点六五八，在真实世界情境中为零点一八二至零点四四七；不同层级的模型排名也有所变化。真实世界情境下，识别临床信息的平均得分为零点七七零，明显高于匹配证据的零点一六五和制定建议的零点一六零。自动评分与医师多数意见的一致性较高，宏平均精确率与召回率综合指标为零点九三零，百分之九十五置信区间为零点九一五至零点九四五；但在证据应用方面，一致性较低。

Conclusion: 研究表明，能够回忆指南知识并不意味着模型能够在患者特定情境中有效检索和应用证据。该基准提供了可追溯的评估框架，但研究结果来自中文材料，且不能据此证明大语言模型具备临床有效性。

Abstract: Benchmark scores for medical large language models (LLMs) are difficult to interpret when scoring criteria cannot be traced to clinical evidence. Here we developed Med Evidence Bench (MEB; https://www.medevidencebench.org.cn/), comprising 1,536 physician-constructed items across three levels: guideline knowledge, standardized clinical scenarios and real-world laboratory scenarios. Each criterion stating a medical claim is linked to a versioned source-text span. Across ten LLMs, the Evidence Trace Score (ETS) was 0.975-0.997 for guideline knowledge, 0.292-0.658 for standardized scenarios and 0.182-0.447 for real-world scenarios, and rankings varied across levels. For real-world scenarios, scores for identifying clinical information (mean, 0.770) exceeded those for matching evidence (0.165) or formulating recommendations (0.160). On 1,296 criterion-level judgments, automated grading agreed with the majority vote of three physicians (macro-F1, 0.930; 95% confidence interval, 0.915-0.945), although agreement was lower for evidence application. These findings from Chinese-language materials indicate that guideline recall does not ensure patient-specific evidence use and do not establish clinical effectiveness.

</details>

*Jifan Gao,Junjie Hu,John Caskey,Madeline Oguss,Simona Cristea,Anoop Mayampurath,Matthew Churpek,Majid Afshar,Guanhua Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需概念标注的临床文本概念解释框架，在保持接近黑箱模型预测性能的同时，提供队列和患者层面的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念解释能以人类可理解的高层概念揭示模型预测依据，但现有方法通常依赖人工标注概念是否出现。临床文本标注成本较高，因此研究旨在减少对概念监督的依赖。

Method: 将临床文本和医学概念描述输入预训练语言模型进行编码，再通过定制的交叉注意力瓶颈，估计每个文本词元与各概念的关联程度。汇总概念相关性分数后，使用线性预测层预测临床结局，并据此提供解释。

Result: 在胸部创伤严重程度分层和住院死亡预测两项任务中，该框架的受试者工作特征曲线下面积与对应黑箱模型相差不超过0.02。框架还能展示队列整体的概念重要性及个体患者的概念贡献。

Conclusion: 该方法无需概念出现情况的真实标注，即可实现基于概念的临床文本预测解释，并在维持较强预测性能的同时提升模型透明度。

Abstract: Objective: Concept-based interpretation has emerged as a promising approach for explaining blackbox models using human-understandable, high-level concepts. However, most existing methods require annotated concept labels to train the model to identify concepts in the input, and obtaining such annotations in clinical text is often expensive. This study aims to enable concept-based interpretation for language models without concept supervision on clinical prediction tasks. Methods: We propose a novel framework that enables concept-based interpretation without concept supervision through a tailored cross-attention bottleneck. Clinical text and the text descriptions of medical concepts are first tokenized and embedded by a pretrained language model. A cross-attention module then estimates the presence of each concept for each text token, producing concept relevance scores that capture how concepts are involved throughout the note. These scores are pooled and used in a linear prediction layer to model clinical outcomes in an interpretable manner. Results: The performance of the proposed framework was compared against blackbox models across two tasks: chest trauma severity stratification using a private dataset and an in-hospital mortality prediction task using the publicly available MIMIC-III dataset. The proposed framework achieved AUROC values within 0.02 of the corresponding blackbox models. The framework also enabled cohort- and patient-level interpretation, capturing the overall concept importance across the cohort and patient-specific concept contributions. Conclusion: Our proposed framework realizes concept-based interpretation without requiring ground-truth concept presence labels. It improves the transparency of clinical text predictions while maintaining strong performance in clinical prediction tasks.

</details>


### [30] [Compute the Interactions, Not the Attributes: What a Fact Layer Must Supply in the Chinese Sexagenary System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13597)
*Huixue Han,Jiaying Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，在规则明确的领域中，仅提供静态事实往往不够；模型还需要直接获得经过计算的事实关系，且呈现方式应适配模型与任务领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常与确定性事实层结合，但事实层究竟需要提供哪些内容，缺少系统测量。论文以八字分析所依赖的中国干支系统为例，考察静态属性和组合关系各自对回答准确率的作用。

Method: 研究构建了可通过表格核验的测试集，涵盖十二类共六百八十二项事实，以及一百六十项流年与命盘之间的冲突关系。比较模型在闭卷、提供参考表、逐步提供计算事实等条件下的表现；另以国际象棋局面进行补充实验，并预先锁定模型预测。

Result: 十一种开源权重模型直接作答时，干支日正确率均不超过百分之四，冲突关系正确率均不超过百分之一。提供静态属性没有稳定改善；即使给出完整关系清单，多数模型直接作答仍接近零准确率。探索性地按查询筛选并提供事实后，两种模型准确率升至百分之七十八至九十二，另四种达到百分之九十六至一百。三种专有推理模型在无辅助时可自行算出百分之六十八至九十五的冲突关系。国际象棋实验也显示，属性不能替代关系，且模型会把列出的干扰项错误归因。

Conclusion: 确定性事实层的关键不只是提供基础属性，还应提供模型难以自行组合或计算的交互关系。事实的筛选与呈现方式需要依据模型能力和领域特点设计；静态事实或冗长的关系清单本身并不保证正确推理。

Abstract: Language-model systems in rule-governed domains often pair the model with a deterministic fact layer, but which facts that layer should supply is rarely measured. We measure it in the Chinese sexagenary system underlying BaZi chart analysis, where every fact has one table-checkable value: 682 items over twelve fact categories and 160 transit--natal collision items, posed closed-book, with the reference table, and with computed facts at increasing levels. In eleven open-weight models answering directly, errors concentrate where attributes must be combined: none gets more than 4\% of sexagenary days or 1\% of collisions right. Static attributes have no consistent effect, and the complete relation list leaves most direct-answering models near zero although it contains the answer. A query-filtered layer (exploratory) raises Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-32B to 78--92\% and four further models to 96--100\%. Three proprietary reasoning models compute 68--95\% of collisions unaided. On chess positions, under predictions locked in advance, attributes again do not substitute for relations and listed distractors are misattributed. The fact layer must supply computed interactions; how they are presented depends on the model and the domain.

</details>


### [31] [One Byte, One Token: Byteifying a 27B Hybrid-Attention LLM with Self-Generated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13607)
*Liwei Zhang,ZhiMing Tong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将一个已训练的二百七十亿参数子词模型直接转换为纯字节级模型，仅缩减词表并继续训练，保留其余权重。转换后多数综合能力大致维持，中文字符级任务明显提升，但中文知识查询能力和生成效率有所下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字节级词元能让模型直接处理文字的拼写与字符结构，但常见的字节对编码会把中文切分成多个字节或字符片段，使拼写信息不易被模型直接利用。本文探讨：能否不增加补丁结构或局部编码器，直接把已有大型子词模型转换成纯字节模型，同时保留大部分能力。

Method: 研究以原有二百七十亿参数模型为起点，将词表从二十四万八千三百二十项缩减至三百八十四项，包括二百五十六个字节符号及特殊符号，其余模型权重保持不变。随后采用先学习编码、再学习阅读的两阶段训练流程，在四点七七吉字节的监督数据上继续训练。对话与行为监督由原模型按自身策略生成，涵盖字词典、一般对话和编程；知识阅读数据则使用百科文本。训练耗时约一百七十八点六小时，使用八张高性能图形处理器。研究还分析并修正了字节模型的贪婪生成失控、采样破坏有效字节序列、惩罚项效果不佳和重复生成等问题。

Result: 在数学、代码生成、多学科知识问答及非思考模式的数学应用题等测试中，转换模型与原模型的差距落在配对自助法置信区间内；但在中文考试、思考模式数学应用题和非思考模式指令遵循测试中下降约四至六分。非思考模式下，模型在各项字符级任务上均领先：字符任务成绩由七十五点二升至八十四点零，中文字符执行任务由八十一点六升至九十三点九，自建中文字符测试由五十四点四升至六十四点零。与此同时，汉字读音、笔画数等查询知识有所退化，思考模式也未能弥补。每步处理速度虽相同，但每步生成的内容减少至原来的约五分之一至三分之一；中文字符生成速度约为原模型的四分之一，处理六万四千字节的输入前缀则慢六点五倍。

Conclusion: 实验表明，直接将大型子词模型转换为纯字节级模型具有可行性，且无需改动主体权重即可保留多数能力，并增强字符级处理表现。但这种转换并非无损：部分中文知识与指令遵循能力下降，长输入处理和中文生成效率也显著变差。因此，纯字节建模适用于重视字符处理的场景；若要用于通用高效服务，还需进一步改善知识保留与推理吞吐量。

Abstract: Byte-level BPE fragments Chinese text (for Qwen3.8 on Chinese Wikipedia, 3.84 bytes per token on average; 64% of tokens are single characters) and hides spelling from the model. We ask whether an already-trained 27B subword model can be converted directly into a pure byte-level model (one byte, one token), without a patching structure or a local encoder, while keeping most of its ability. Starting from Qwen3.8-27B (48 Gated DeltaNet layers + 16 full-attention layers), we shrink the vocabulary from 248,320 to 384 entries (the 256 byte symbols already in the BPE vocabulary plus special ids), leave every other weight untouched, and continue training in a two-stage curriculum (encoding first, then reading) on 4.77 GB of supervised bytes: all dialogue/behavioral supervision is generated on-policy by the original model itself (character dictionary, word dictionary, general dialogue, coding sessions), and knowledge reading uses encyclopedia text (54% of supervised bytes). Training takes 178.6 h on 8xH200. On the same GPU and serving stack, the byte model's gap to the original falls within the paired bootstrap interval on MATH-500, HumanEval+, MMLU-Pro and non-thinking GSM8K, and it loses 4-6 points on C-Eval, thinking-mode GSM8K and non-thinking IFEval. Without thinking it leads on every character-level task (CUTE 75.2 -> 84.0, EXECUTE-zho 81.6 -> 93.9, our Chinese character suite 54.4 -> 64.0), but lookup knowledge about Chinese characters such as pronunciation and stroke count drops, and thinking does not recover it. The cost is throughput: per-step speed is identical, but each step yields 3-5x less output; Chinese characters per second is about 1/4 of the original, and prefill at 64K bytes is 6.5x slower. We also document four byte-model failure modes (greedy runaway, sampling breaking UTF-8, ineffective penalty terms on a small vocabulary, repetition) and their fixes.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乌兹别克语正处于文字转换阶段，西里尔字母和拉丁字母常在同页甚至同一句中混用。两种字母表中视觉相同或相近的字符容易造成光学字符识别错误；混合文字页面的形近字符错误密度约为单一文字页面的五倍。此外，工程地质和信息通信基础设施报告等语料不得离开机构，要求处理流程完全在本地完成。

Method: 系统结合字节级文本到文本模型、依据三百五十九条人工分类错误事件估计采样权重的噪声生成器，以及受莱文斯坦距离约束的保守解码规则。该设计针对形近字符错误进行训练，并限制纠错时引入过多改动；所有环节均在本地硬件上执行。

Result: 在包含五千二百零四组文本行对的金标准测试集上，字符错误率由百分之五点七八降至百分之三点六九；形近字符恢复率达到百分之九十一点三，同时仅新增八十四处错误。

Conclusion: 该方法表明，结合针对性噪声建模与保守解码，能够有效纠正混合文字乌兹别克语文档中的形近字符错误，并满足敏感语料本地处理的需求。

Abstract: In Uzbek official and technical documents, a large share of OCR errors is caused not by poor image quality but by characters that are visually identical across the two alphabets in use. Because the script transition is still under way, Cyrillic and Latin appear on the same page and often within a single sentence, and in our measurements the density of homoglyph errors on such pages is close to five times higher than in single-script text. We study this problem in a setting where the corpus of engineering-geological and ICT infrastructure reports may not leave the organisation, so every stage was executed on local hardware. The proposed system combines a byte-level ByT5 model with a noise generator whose sampling weights are estimated from 359 manually classified error events, together with a Levenshtein-bounded conservative decoding rule. On a Gold set of 5,204 line pairs it lowers the character error rate from 0.0578 to 0.0369, restores 91.3% of the homoglyphs and introduces only 84 new errors.

</details>


### [33] [Synapse-Control: Making Agent Experience Actionable with Lightweight Runtime Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13615)
*Zhiyuan Huang,Shengan Zheng,Chenghu Yang,Song Zhang,Lingyun Li,Chen Yu,Weiquan Huang,Linpeng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 突触控制通过规则筛选、轻量级建议分类器、微型契约和提示价值表，减少经验复用中的生成式诊断开销，同时提升复杂工具任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体可以复用以往执行经验来提高复杂工具任务的可靠性，但现有系统往往先调用大型模型生成失败诊断，再据此检索建议，增加了推理成本和控制耗时。研究旨在以更轻量的控制机制完成建议筛选与发送时机判断。

Method: 突触控制采用轻量级控制平面：先用规则缩小相关建议范围，再用经教师标注的状态—建议样本微调的17亿参数分类器为候选建议打分，取代生成式诊断。系统还以微型契约记录可复用的接口检查，例如必需参数和有效选项，而非规定完整任务程序；提示价值表则记录既往指导及反馈，并通过冷却机制和令牌预算检查减少重复建议。

Result: 在应用世界和网络商店两项基准上，每种方法进行1308次尝试。突触控制共取得270次成功，较基线多29.8%；同时替代了4987次生成式诊断调用。相较哨兵系统，其辅助控制耗时在两项基准上分别降低50.2%和51.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将建议筛选、时机控制与轻量级经验表示相结合，可以减少对大型模型诊断调用的依赖，并在提升任务成功率的同时降低控制成本。

Abstract: Large language model agents reuse execution experience to improve reliability on complex tool-based tasks. Systems such as Sentry call large models to generate failure diagnoses before retrieving advice, adding inference cost to experience reuse. We present Synapse-Control, an agent runtime whose lightweight control plane selects advice and determines when to send it to the task-executing model. Rules shortlist relevant advice, and a lightweight 1.7B classifier fine-tuned on teacher-labeled state-suggestion pairs scores those candidates instead of generating a diagnosis. Micro-contracts encode reusable interface checks, such as required arguments or valid options, without prescribing complete task programs. A Prompt Value Table records earlier guidance and feedback to limit repeated suggestions through cooldown and token-budget checks. Across 1,308 attempts per method in AppWorld and WebShop, Synapse-Control achieves 270 successes (29.8% more than Base), replaces 4,987 generative diagnosis calls, and reduces auxiliary control time relative to Sentry by 50.2% and 51.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [34] [An Interpretable Stylometric Framework for Early Chinese Buddhist Translator Styles: A Case Study on the Attribution of Disputed Works of An Shigao](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13636)
*Yating Pan,Xinyue Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出一种可解释的文体计量框架，用于判断早期汉译佛经的译者归属；结果显示，编号一五五七和编号零一零一与安世高译作的风格最为接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期汉译佛经的译者归属需要有可追溯至具体文本的证据。既有判断若缺乏透明、可核查的语言依据，学者便难以评估归属结论，因此需要一种便于解释和检验的方法。

Method: 研究从七位东汉至西晋译者的三十一部已确认译作中建立文体档案，并选取八部归属有争议的文本与安世高的文体进行比较。框架采用由佛教学者界定或审阅的语言特征，包括虚词、句式套语、句末字、句长和词性词组序列；无需针对具体任务训练模型，并可检查哪些特征支持归属判断。

Result: 八部争议文本中，编号一五五七和编号零一零一与已确认的安世高译作最为相似，得分分别为零点七八三和零点六八七。所得归属判断与一项已发表的、基于双向编码器的研究对同一批文本给出的判断一致。

Conclusion: 该框架为早期汉译佛经译者归属提供了可解释、无需专门训练且便于学者复核的文体证据。结果支持将编号一五五七和编号零一零一视为最接近安世高译作的文本，但文体相似度应作为归属研究的证据之一，而非单独的定论。

Abstract: Attributing early Chinese Buddhist translations requires evidence that scholars can trace back to the texts. We develop an interpretable stylometric framework that uses features defined or reviewed by Buddhist philologists, including function characters, sentence formulas, sentence-final characters, sentence length, and part-of-speech n-grams. We construct stylistic profiles from 31 authenticated translations by seven translators from the Eastern Han through the Western Jin, then compare eight disputed texts with An Shigao's profile. Two texts, T1557 and T0101, show the strongest stylistic similarity to the authenticated An Shigao corpus, with scores of 0.783 and 0.687, respectively. The attributions agree with those reported by a published BERT-based approach on the same set of disputed texts. The framework requires no task-specific training and allows scholars to inspect which linguistic features support each proposed attribution.

</details>


### [35] [OpticalRAG: Pixel-Space Compression for Token-Efficient Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13675)
